# Functional audit control and callback register

Baseline source occurrence index, supporting [workflow inventory](APP_WIDE_FUNCTIONAL_INVENTORY.md), [Settings](SETTINGS_FUNCTIONAL_INVENTORY.md), [Network](NETWORK_FILES_FUNCTIONAL_INVENTORY.md). It includes registration sites and callbacks, including focus/key/lifecycle-adjacent dispatch. Multiple occurrences can represent one action, generated rows can share one listener, and commented examples can be present: **this is not a visible-control count**. Reachability of platform/classic callbacks remains conditional; no missing direct Preview reference is evidence of removability. A source link identifies the full handler; domain columns map common effect/persistence risk.
